| Glyph Prism | `/Lotus/Types/Restoratives/Consumable/GlyphConsumableNoCharges` | ExportGear | [page](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/Glyph) | **Lead acquisition statement:** {{Infobox<br>\| Box title=Glyph<br>\| image=SampleInGameGlyph.jpg<br>\| caption=Glyph as seen in-game (code: WARFRAMEWIKI)<br>}}<br>'''Glyphs''' are [[wikipedia:Pin Art\|pin-table]] style icons that can be placed on surfaces during missions to display a pre-selected image and also serve as player icons/avatars in the game.<br><br>{{toclimit\|3}}<br><br>==Changing your Glyph==<br>You can change your equipped glyph by opening the pause menu, selecting '''Profile''', then selecting '''Glyph.'''<br>==Default Glyphs==<br>These Glyphs are unlocked by default:<br>{{GlyphBox\|ExcaliburGlyph.png\|Excalibur Glyph}}<br>{{GlyphBox\|LotusSymbolGlyph.png\|Lotus Symbol Glyph}}<br>{{GlyphBox\|RhinoGlyph.png\|Rhino Glyph}}<br>{{GlyphBox\|TrinityGlyph.png\|Trinity Glyph}}<br>{{GlyphBox\|VoltGlyph.png\|Volt Glyph}}<br><br>Although these Glyphs are not unlocked by default, they share the same design and can be purchased for {{pc\|10}} each.<br><br>{{GlyphBox\|AshGlyph.png\|Ash Glyph}}<br>{{GlyphBox\|EmberGlyph.png\|Ember Glyph}}<br>{{GlyphBox\|LokiGlyph.png\|Loki Glyph}}<br>{{GlyphBox\|MagGlyph.png\|Mag Glyph}}<br><br>==Normal Glyphs==<br>These Glyphs depict Warframes and their Alternate Helmets with a light/dark background. They can be purchased for {{pc\|10}} each. Unlike other Warframe cosmetics like Badges and Emblems, Sigils offer a wide range of customization, allowing players to adjust their size, position and orientation, and players can choose to install them either on the front or the back of the Warframe. As you take damage to your health the sigil will become more visible.<br>{{SigilBox<br>\| mode        = stack<br>\| image       = BloodiedSigil.png<br>\| name        = Bloodshed Sigil<br>\| description = With this sigil, damage will leave you blood-soaked, while in Captura your Warframe stands wreathed in the gore of its enemies. Honor the Maiden of Death by donning her traditional garb.<br>}}<br><br>The '''Leaping Thrasher Sigil''' is a rare drop from the [[Leaping Thrasher]] enemy. Originally only found during the Emissary Derelict missions during Nightwave Season 2, Leaping Thrashers can now be found in the Derelict Emissary Assassinate mission.  Leaping Thrashers can also spawn during Shrine Defense missions.<br>{{SigilBox<br>\| image       = LeapingThrasherSigil.png<br>\| link        = Leaping Thrasher<br>\| name        = Leaping Thrasher Sigil<br>\| description = A sigil depicting a Leaping Thrasher.<br>}}<br><br>The '''Scar Sigil''' is a reward for completing [[The Second Dream]] quest.<br>{{SigilBox<br>\| mode        = stack<br>\| image       = ScarSigil.png<br>\| name        = Scar Sigil<br>\| description = A memento for those who have awoken from the Second Dream.<br>}}<br><br>===[[Adversary System\|Adversary]] Sigils===<br>The '''Old Blood Sigil''' is awarded to players the first time they convert their [[Kuva Lich]].<br>{{SigilBox<br>\| mode        = stack<br>\| image       = OldBloodSigil.png<br>\| name        = Old Blood Sigil<br>\| description = The sigil of one who has bested an inhuman adversary born of the Old Blood: a Kuva Lich.<br>}}<br><br>The '''Sisterhood Sigil''' is awarded to players the first time they convert their [[Sisters of Parvos\|Sister of Parvos]].<br>{{SigilBox<br>\| mode        = stack<br>\| image       = SisterhoodSigil.png<br>\| name        = Sisterhood Sigil<br>\| description = A mark of respect for the Sisters of Parvos.<br>}}<br><br>The '''Converted Coda Sigil''' is awarded to players the first time they convert their [[Technocyte Coda]]. The '''Vanquished Coda Sigil''' is awarded to players the first time they vanquish one.<br>{{SigilBox<br>\| image       = ConvertedCodaSigil.png<br>\| name        = Converted Coda Sigil<br>\| description = The show must go on. This sigil marks the conversion of a hostile Technocyte Coda into a Techrot friend.<br>}}<br>{{SigilBox<br>\| image       = VanquishedCodaSigil.png<br>\| name        = Vanquished Coda Sigil<br>\| description = The show’s over. This sigil marks the end of a Technocyte Coda’s career of music and mayhem.<br>}}<br><br>==Daily Tribute Milestone Sigils==<br>The following sigils are exclusive to the [[Daily Tribute]] system.<br><br>The '''Imminent Eclipse Sigil''' will become available every 100 days, beginning at day 50, until chosen as the Milestone reward.<br>{{SigilBox<br>\| mode        = stack<br>\| image As with his other wares, it will take several visits before a particular Sigil reappears in his stock.<br><br>The '''Prisma Sigil''' can be acquired for {{cc\|50,000}} and visage.<br>}}<br><br>==Arbitration Sigils==<br>The '''Scars of Arbitration Sigil''' is currently purchasable in the Arbitration Honors store for 20 [[Vitus Essence]].<br>{{SigilBox<br>\| mode        = stack<br>\| image       = ScarsofArbitrationSigil.png<br>\| name        = Scars of Arbitration Sigil<br>\| description = Bear the scars earned in deadly Arbitrations.<br>}}<br><br>==Purchasable Sigils==<br>The '''Mastery Sigil''' dynamically changes design based on the player's [[Mastery Rank#Mastery Ranks\|Mastery Rank]]. It was originally distributed to players who logged in from November 14, 2014 to November 26, 2014, but has since been made available from the Market for {{cc\|1}} in {{ver\|17.2.5}}.<br>{{SigilBox<br>\| mode        = stack<br>\| image       = MasterySigilR.png<br>\| name        = Mastery Sigil<br>\| description = This sigil represents the pinnacle of mastery within the Tenno.<br>}}<br><br>'''Community of Tenno''' can be purchased from the Market for {{cc\|1}}.<br>{{SigilBox<br>\| mode        = stack<br>\| image       = CommunityOfTenno_Market.png<br>\| name        = Community of Tenno<br>\| description = Dedicated to the amazing Warframe community.<br>}}<br><br>The '''Festive Sigil''' is a limited-edition Sigil that was available from the [[Winter Bundle]] during December 2014, 2015, 2016, and 2017.<br>{{SigilBox<br>\| mode        = stack<br>\| image       = FestiveSigil.png<br>\| name        = Festive Sigil<br>\| description = A sigil containing a festive design.<br>}}<br><br>The '''Banshee Soprana Sigil''' is available as part of the [[Banshee Soprana Collection]] for {{pc\|250}}.<br>{{SigilBox<br>\| mode        = stack<br>\| image       = BansheeDeluxeSigil.png<br>\| name        = Banshee Soprana Sigil<br>\| description = Wear Banshee Soprana's sigil with pride.<br>}}<br><br>The '''Wukong Qitian Sigil''' is available in the [[Market]] for {{pc\|15}} or as part of the [[Wukong Qitian Collection]] for {{pc\|295}}.<br>{{SigilBox<br>\| mode        = stack<br>\| image       = WukongQitianSigil.png<br>\| name        = Wukong Qitian Sigil<br>\| description = Show due respect for Wukong Qitian’s power and cunning with this sigil.<br>}}<br><br>===[[Lunar Renewal]] Sigils===<br>The '''Lunar Renewal Prowl Sigil''' was available as part of the [[Lunar Renewal Celebration Pack]] for {{pc\|105}} or [[Luminous Lunar Renewal Collection]] for {{pc\|275}} from February 15th, 2022 to March 7th, 2022. It is available for {{pc\|40}} during the [[Lunar New Year]].<br>{{SigilBox<br>\| mode        = stack<br>\| image       = LunarRenewalProwlSigil.png<br>\| name        = Lunar Renewal Prowl Sigil<br>\| description = Face the coming year with the boldness of the legendary tiger.<br>}}<br><br>The '''Lunar Renewal Swift Sigil''' is available from the market during the [[Lunar New Year]] for {{pc\|40}} or as part of the [[Garnet Renewal Bundle]] for {{pc\|265}}.<br>{{SigilBox<br>\| mode        = stack<br>\| image       = LunarRenewalSwiftSigil.png<br>\| name        = Lunar Renewal Swift Sigil<br>\| description = Celebrate Lunar Renewal with the kind heart of the gentle rabbit.<br>}}<br><br>The '''Lunar Renewal Soar Sigil''' is available from the market during the [[Lunar New Year]] for {{pc\|40}} or as part of the [[Fire Agate Bundle]] for {{pc\|295}}.<br>{{SigilBox<br>\| mode        = stack<br>\| image       = LunarRenewalSoarSigil.png<br>\| name        = Lunar Renewal Soar Sigil<br>\| description = Soar into the coming year with the power of the honourable dragon.<br>}}<br><br>The '''Lunar Renewal Snake Sigil''' is available from the market during the [[Lunar New Year]] for {{pc\|40}} or as part of either the [[Smoky Quartz Bundle]] for {{pc\|215}} or the [[Noble Opal Collection]] for {{pc\|805}}.<br>{{SigilBox<br>\| mode        = stack<br>\| image       = LunarRenewalSnakeSigil.png<br>\| name        = Lunar Renewal Snake Sigil<br>\| description = Transform in the coming year with the intuition of the elegant snake.<br>}}<br><br>The '''Lunar Renewal Horse Sigil''' is available from the market during the [[Lunar New Year]] for {{pc\|40}} or as part of either the [[Year Of The Kaithe Collection]] for {{pc\|295}} or the [[Lustrous Lunar Renewal Collection]] for {{pc\|700}}.<br>{{SigilBox<br>\| mode        = stack<br>\| image       = LunarRenewalHorseSigil.png<br>\| name        = Lunar Renewal Horse Sigil<br>\| description = Gallop into the coming year with the freedom of the loyal horse.<br>}}<br><br>===Deluxe Sigils===<br>These sigils, rather than being a single emblem on the chest or back, are full-body effects similar to the Bloodshed Sigil.<br><br>The '''Vaporis Sigil''' is available in the [[Market]] for {{pc\|25}} or as part of the [[Lavos Khymia Collection]] for {{pc\|425}}.<br>{{SigilBox<br>\| mode        = stack<br>\| image       = VaporisSigil.png<br>\| name        = Vaporis Sigil<br>\| description = The touch of alchemy leaves its mark, permeating the entire body. Lavos Khymia's signature sigil.<br>}}<br><br>The '''Kickback Sigil''' is available in the [[Market]] for {{pc\|25}}, as part of the [[Gauss Moto Collection]] for {{pc\|390}}, or as part of the [[Eat & Run Collection]] for {{pc\|855}}.<br>{{SigilBox<br>\| mode        = stack<br>\| image       = KickbackSigil.png<br>\| name        = Kickback Sigil<br>\| description = High speed movement can leave its mark. Gauss Moto's signature sigil.<br>}}<br><br>The '''Road Rash Sigil''' is available in the [[Market]] for {{pc\|25}} or as part of the [[Eat & Run Collection]] for {{pc\|855}}.<br>{{SigilBox<br>\| mode        = stack<br>\| image       = RoadRashSigil.png<br>\| name        = Road Rash Sigil<br>\| description = This sigil won't just buff out.<br>}}<br><br>===[[Heirloom Skins\|Heirloom]] Sigils===<br>The '''Deca Heirloom Sigil''' was part of the [[Risen Heirloom Collection]] or [[Zenith Heirloom Collection]], sold for real-world currency for a limited time. These bundles have since been retired and are no longer available for purchase.<br>{{SigilBox<br>\| mode        = stack<br>\| image       = DecaHeirloomSigil.png<br>\| name        = Deca Heirloom Sigil<br>\| description = A sigil that celebrates the Tenno’s loyalty and dedication.<br>}}<br><br>The '''Ember Heirloom Sigil''' is available in the [[Market]] for {{pc\|25}} or as part of the [[Ember Heirloom Singe Collection]] for {{pc\|400}}.<br>{{SigilBox<br>\| mode        = stack<br>\| image       = EmberHeirloomSigil.png<br>\| name        = Ember Heirloom Sigil<br>\| description = A sigil that celebrates Ember's legacy.<br>}}<br><br>The '''Mesa Heirloom Sigil''' is available in the [[Market]] for {{pc\|25}} or as part of the [[Mesa Heirloom Collection]] for {{pc\|400}}.<br>{{SigilBox<br>\| mode        = stack<br>\| image       = MesaHeirloomSigil.png<br>\| name        = Mesa Heirloom Sigil<br>\| description = Mesa Heirloom's sigil warns outlaws their time has come.<br>}}<br><br>The '''Rhino Heirloom Sigil''' is available in the [[Market]] for {{pc\|25}} or as part of the [[Rhino Heirloom Iron Collection]] for {{pc\|400}}.<br>{{SigilBox<br>\| mode        = stack<br>\| image       = RhinoHeirloomSigil.png<br>\| name        = Rhino Heirloom Sigil<br>\| description = A sigil that celebrates Rhino's legacy.<br>}}<br><br>The '''Valkyr Heirloom Sigil''' is available in the [[Market]] for {{pc\|25}} or as part of the [[Valkyr Heirloom Collection]] for {{pc\|400}}.<br>{{SigilBox<br>\| mode        = stack<br>\| image       = ValkyrHeirloomSigil.png<br>\| name        = Valkyr Heirloom Sigil<br>\| description = A sigil that celebrates Valkyr’s legacy.<br>}}<br><br>The '''Vauban Heirloom Sigil''' is available in the [[Market]] for {{pc\|25}} or as part of the [[Vauban Heirloom Collection]] for {{pc\|400}}.<br>{{SigilBox<br>\| mode        = stack<br>\| image       = VaubanHeirloomSigil.png<br>\| name        = Vauban Heirloom Sigil<br>\| description = A sigil that celebrates Vauban’s legacy.<br>}}<br><br>==[[Prime Access]] Sigils==<br>The '''Velorum Prime Sigil''' was originally part of [[Prime Access#Nova Prime\|Nova Prime Access]], available from purchasing the Molecular or Accessories pack. It can be purchased from [[Prime Resurgence]] when {{WF\|Nova Prime}} is in rotation for 1 {{Resource\|Regal Aya}}.<br>{{SigilBox<br>\| mode        = stack<br>\| image       = VerlorumPrimeSigil.png<br>\| name        = Velorum Prime Sigil<br>\| description = Champions of the Orokin were allowed to wear this Sigil as proof of their loyalty.<br>}}<br><br>The '''Cycuta Prime Sigil''' was originally part of [[Prime Access#Saryn Prime\|Saryn Prime Access]], available from purchasing the Miasma or Accessories pack. It can be purchased from [[Prime Resurgence]] when {{WF\|Saryn Prime}} is in rotation for 1 {{Resource\|Regal Aya}}.<br>{{SigilBox<br>\| mode        = stack<br>\| image       = CycutaPrimeSigil.png<br>\| name        = Cycuta Prime Sigil<br>\| description = This ancient glyph was the focus of an ancient Tenno meditation ritual.<br>}}<br><br>The '''Rift Walker Prime Sigil''' was originally part of [[Prime Access#Limbo Prime\|Limbo Prime Access]], available from purchasing the Cataclysm or Accessories pack. It can be purchased from [[Prime Resurgence]] when {{WF\|Limbo Prime}} is in rotation for 1 {{Resource\|Regal Aya}}.<br>{{SigilBox<br>\| mode        = stack<br>\| image       = RiftWalkerPrimeSigil.png<br>\| name        = Rift Walker Prime Sigil<br>\| description = The signature sigil of Limbo Prime.<br>}}<br><br>==Platinum Pack Sigils==<br>The '''Nava Sigil''' was available from a previous iteration of WARFRAME's [https://www.warframe.com/starterpack Starter Pack], which was discontinued on June 25th, 2019.<br>{{SigilBox<br>\| mode        = stack<br>\| image       = NavaSigil.png<br>\| name        = Nava Sigil<br>\| description = Pay respect to The Lotus with this elegant sigil.<br>}}<br><br>The '''Kumuda Sigil''' was available from June 25th, 2019 to August 25th, 2020 from a previous Starter Pack.<br>{{SigilBox<br>\| mode        = stack<br>\| image       = KumudaSigil.png<br>\| name        = Kumuda Sigil<br>\| description = Pay respect to The Lotus with this elegant sigil.<br>}}<br><br>The '''Empyrean Sigil''' was available from December 12th, 2019 to August 25th, 2020 from the Empyrean Supporter Pack.<br>{{SigilBox<br>\| mode        = stack<br>\| image       = EmpyreanSigil.png<br>\| name        = Empyrean Sigil<br>\| description = The same sigil that was proudly worn by Old War Railjack crews.<br>}}<br><br>==Twitch Game Store and Twitch Prime Sigils==<br>The '''Prominence Sigil''' was obtainable by purchasing the Prominence Bundle II from the Twitch Game Store. This bundle was discontinued on November 27th, 2018. It was later made available from [[Varzia]] for 5 {{Resource\|Aya}}.<br>{{SigilBox<br>\| mode        = stack<br>\| image       = ProminenceSigil.png<br>\| name        = Prominence Sigil<br>\| description = A sigil depicting bold geometric patterns.<br>}}<br><br>The '''Static Reactor Prime Sigil''' was available as Twitch Prime loot from Feb 18th – Apr 7th, 2020. It was later made available from [[Varzia]] for 1 {{Resource\|Regal Aya}}.<br>{{SigilBox<br>\| mode        = stack<br>\| image       = StaticReactorPrimeSigil.png<br>\| name        = Static Reactor Prime Sigil<br>\| description = Paired with this sigil, a Warframe will flicker and flash like a thunderhead.<br>}}<br><br>The '''Verv Sigil''' was available as Twitch Prime loot from Apr 5th – May 17th, 2022. It was later made available from [[Varzia]] for 5 {{Resource\|Aya}}.<br>{{SigilBox<br>\| mode        = stack<br>\| image       = VervSigil.png<br>\| name        = Verv Sigil<br>\| description = A compelling badge of honor for the Tenno.<br>}}<br><br>The '''Necraloid Sigil''' was available from Prime Gaming around October 2020. It was later made available from [[Varzia]] for 10 {{Resource\|Aya}} in a bundle with the Necraloid Glyph.<br>{{SigilBox<br>\| mode        = stack<br>\| image       = NecraloidSigil.png<br>\| name        = Necraloid Sigil<br>\| description = Are you as wormed as the others?<br>}}<br><br>==Event Sigils==<br>These Sigils could only be obtained by participating in their associated [[:Category:Event\|Event]].<br><br>The '''False Profit Sigil''' was available during [[Operation: False Profit]]. It was awarded for achieving a score of 100 points or more during the event.<br>{{SigilBox<br>\| mode        = stack<br>\| image       = RiotMoaEventBadge(SxWhite).png<br>\| link        = Operation: False Profit<br>\| name        = False Profit Sigil<br>\| description = This sigil commemorates the Tenno victory over the machinations of Nef Anyo.<br>}}<br><br>The '''Acolyte Sigil''' was available during [[Operation: Shadow Debt]]. It was awarded for collecting at least 4 Cloaking Data.<br>{{SigilBox<br>\| mode        = stack<br>\| image       = AcolyteSigil(SxWhite).png<br>\| link        = Operation: Shadow Debt<br>\| name        = Acolyte Sigil<br>\| description = Never forget those who would defile the Tenno ways.<br>}}<br><br>The '''Rathuum Sigil''' was available during [[Operation: Rathuum]]. It was awarded for collecting at least 10 Judgment Points.<br>{{SigilBox<br>\| mode        = stack<br>\| image       = RathuumSigil(SxWhite).png<br>\| link        = Operation: Rathuum<br>\| name        = Rathuum Sigil<br>\| description = A sigil commemorating the liberation of defectors from the Grineer.<br>}}<br><br>'''The Index Sigil''' was available during [[The Index\|The Index Preview]]. It was awarded for achieving a score of 100 or more in a High Risk match.<br>{{SigilBox<br>\| mode        = stack<br>\| image       = TheIndexSigil(SxWhite).png<br>\| link        = The Index<br>\| name        = The Index Sigil<br>\| description = A memento of The Index.<br>}}<br><br>The '''Quick Steel Sigil''' was a reward from the first phase of the [[Conclave\|Quick Steel]] Conclave alert in February 2017.<br>{{SigilBox<br>\| mode        = stack<br>\| image       = QuickSteelSigil(SxWhite).png<br>\| name        = Quick Steel Sigil<br>\| description = A symbol of pride for those that braved blade and star in The Conclave.<br>}}<br><br>The '''Ambulas Reborn Sigil''' was available during [[Operation: Ambulas Reborn]]. It could be purchased for 75 Animo Beacons.<br>{{SigilBox<br>\| mode        = stack<br>\| image       = AmbulasRebornEmblem(SxWhite).png<br>\| link        = Operation: Ambulas Reborn<br>\| name        = Ambulas Reborn Sigil<br>\| description = A sigil commemorating the fight against the Ambulas incursion.<br>}}<br><br>The '''Buried Debts Sigil''' was originally released during [[Operation: Buried Debts]], and is now available from the periodically recurring [[Thermia Fractures]] event. It is awarded for reaching 75 total fractures sealed.<br>{{SigilBox<br>\| mode        = stack<br>\| image       = BuriedDebtsSigil(SxWhite).png<br>\| link        = Operation: Buried Debts<br>\| name        = Buried Debts Sigil<br>\| description = For those who have performed great works, safeguarding Fortuna against the ravages of Exploiter and her thermia fractures.<br>}}<br><br>The '''Rhino Palatine Sigil''' was available from a [[Gift from the Lotus]] [[Alert]] that ran from May 8 to May 15, 2019, and now can be purchased from [[Baro Ki'Teer]] for {{cc\|55,000}} and {{dc\|45}}.<br>{{SigilBox<br>\| mode        = stack<br>\| image       = RhinoPalatineSigil(SxWhite).png<br>\| name        = Rhino Palatine Sigil<br>\| description = Pay homage to warriors of a more chivalrous time with this sigil.<br>}}<br><br>The '''Hostile Mergers Sigil''' was awarded for completing the second mission of [[Operation: Hostile Mergers]].<br>{{SigilBox<br>\| mode        = stack<br>\| image       = HostileMergersSigil(SxWhite).png<br>\| link        = Operation: Hostile Mergers<br>\| name        = Hostile Mergers Sigil<br>\| description = This sigil commemorates the Tenno victory over the machinations of Alad V and his Amalgam Project.<br>}}<br><br>These '''Clan Sigils''' were originally available for purchase during [[Operation: Scarlet Spear]], purchased from [[Little Duck]] in the Scarlet Spear Flotilla Relay for 2,000 {{Resource\|Scarlet Credits}} each.<br><br>They have been made available in subsequent events as a recurring reward:<br>* [[Operation: Gargoyle's Cry]]: 3 {{Resource\|Grotesque Splinter\|Grotesque Splinters}} each<br>* [[Operation: Belly of the Beast]]: 15 {{Resource\|Volatile Motes}} each<br>* [[Operation: Eight Claw]]: 9 Sigil<br>}}<br>{{SigilBox<br>\| image = ClanSigilPhased.png<br>\| name  = Phased Clan Sigil<br>}}<br><br>These '''Naberus Sigils''' sigils are available for purchase during the recurring [[Nights of Naberus]] event for 5 {{Resource\|Mother Token\|Mother Tokens}} each.<br>{{SigilBox<br>\| image       = GrimGrinNaberusSigil.png<br>\| name        = Grim Grin Naberus Sigil<br>\| description = A prey.<br>}}<br>{{SigilBox<br>\| image       = SomberStalkerSigil.png<br>\| name        = Somber Stalker Sigil<br>\| description = From the shadows.<br>}}<br>{{SigilBox<br>\| image       = ShroudofDynarSigil.png<br>\| name        = Shroud of Dynar Sigil<br>\| description = A morbidly festive sigil.<br>}}<br><br>These '''Cavia Sigils''' were available for purchase during [[Operation: Gargoyle's Cry]] for 1 {{resource\|Grotesque Splinter}} each. They were subsequently made available during [[Operation: Atramentum]] for 15 {{Resource\|Nightmare Tatters}} each.<br>{{SigilBox<br>\| image = Bird3Sigil(SxWhite).png<br>\| name  = Bird 3 Sigil<br>}}<br>{{SigilBox<br>\| image = FibonacciSigil(SxWhite).png<br>\| name  = Fibonacci Sigil<br>}}<br>{{SigilBox<br>\| image = TagferSigil(SxWhite).png<br>\| name  = Tagfer Sigil<br>}}<br><br>The '''Belly of the Beast Sigil''' was available for purchase during [[Operation: Belly of the Beast]] for 15 {{Resource\|Volatile Motes}}.<br>{{SigilBox<br>\| mode        = stack<br>\| image       = BellyOfTheBeastEmblem(SxWhite).png<br>\| link        = Operation: Belly of the Beast<br>\| name        = Belly of the Beast Sigil<br>\| description = A sigil for the Tenno who played their part in Operation: Belly of the Beast.<br>}}<br><br>The '''Eight Claw Sigil''' was available for purchase during [[Operation: Eight Claw]] for 25 {{Resource\|Dominus Aureus}}. It was subsequently made available during [[Operation: Atramentum]] for 15 {{Resource\|Nightmare Tatters}}.<br>{{SigilBox<br>\| mode        = stack<br>\| image       = EightClawSigil.png<br>\| link        = Operation: Eight Claw<br>\| name        = Eight Claw Sigil<br>\| description = Commemorate the fight against Neci Rusalka for the throne of Duviri during Operation: Eight Claw with this image       = AtramentumSigil(SxWhite).png<br>\| link        = Operation: Atramentum<br>\| name        = Atramentum Sigil<br>\| description = Keep the memory of the Shadowgrapher and Operation: Aspirant Zorba with this sigil.<br>}}<br><br>==Tactical Alert Sigils==<br>This set of animated '''Christmas-themed Sigils''' were individually offered as rewards for a series of [[Tactical Alert]]s running through the month of December 2014.<br>{{SigilBox<br>\| image = XmasSigilD.png<br>\| name  = Evergreen Sigil<br>}}<br>{{SigilBox<br>\| image = XmasSigilB.png<br>\| name  = Nistlebrush Sigil<br>}}<br>{{SigilBox<br>\| image = XmasSigilC.png<br>\| name  = Tolling Sigil<br>}}<br>{{SigilBox<br>\| image = XmasSigilA.png<br>\| name  = Wreath Sigil<br>}}<br><br>The '''Rift Sigil''' was a reward from the ''Escalation'' phases of various [[Tactical Alert]]s, including ''Phoenix Intercept'', ''Emergency Exit'', ''Project Undermine'', and ''Proxy Rebellion''. It was also offered as a rank up reward in [[Nightwave]] Intermission I. This Sigil glows brighter the more energy the Warframe has.<br>{{SigilBox<br>\| mode        = stack<br>\| image       = RiftSigil.png<br>\| name        = Rift Sigil<br>\| description = This sigil is laced with energy from the Void.<br>}}<br><br>The '''Day of the Dead Sigil''' is a limited-edition Sigil rewarded from the [[Hallowed Nightmares]] Tactical Alert.<br>{{SigilBox<br>\| mode        = stack<br>\| image       = DayoftheDeadSigil.png<br>\| name        = Day of the Dead Sigil<br>\| description = A morbidly festive sigil.<br>}}<br><br>The '''Dog Days Sigil''' is available for purchase from [[Nakak]] for 420 {{Resource\|Nakak Pearl\|Nakak Pearls}} during the Dog Days [[Tactical Alert]].<br>{{SigilBox<br>\| mode        = stack<br>\| image       = DogDaysIcon.png<br>\| name        = Dog Days Sigil<br>\| description = Beat the heat with this unique Sigil.<br>}}<br><br>==Community Event Sigils==<br>The '''Blade and Gun''' Sigil was awarded to the winners of the Blade and Gun Challenges that were hosted on the Warframe Forums.<br>{{SigilBox<br>\| mode        = stack<br>\| image       = BladeAndGunSigil(SxWhite).png<br>\| name        = Blade and Gun Sigil<br>\| description = Awarded to dedicated members within the Warframe community.<br>}}<br><br>The '''Winter Solstice Sigil''' was given to all Tenno during December 2016 after achieving the first donation milestone of Tennobaum.<br>{{SigilBox<br>\| mode        = stack<br>\| image       = WinterSolsticeSigil.png<br>\| name        = Winter Solstice Sigil<br>\| description = A festive sigil.<br>}}<br><br>The '''Teralyst Prominence Sigil''' was awarded during The Great Eidolon Hunt, an event on Twitch commemorating the release of the Plains of Eidolon. It was obtained by watching a Warframe stream while the streamer completed achievement "By the Dawn’s Early Light".<br>{{SigilBox<br>\| mode        = stack<br>\| image       = TeralystSigil.png<br>\| name        = Teralyst Prominence Sigil<br>\| description = A bold sigil depicting a Sentient Teralyst.<br>}}<br><br>The '''Ostron Community Sigil''' was gifted to all players on PC who logged in from 25th of October to 1st of November 2017 as a thank you for testing and providing feedback throughout [[Update 22]].<br>{{SigilBox<br>\| mode        = stack<br>\| image       = OstronCommunityGlyph.png<br>\| name        = Ostron Community Sigil<br>\| description = A symbol of Ostron appreciation.<br>}}<br><br>The '''Trailer Entrant Sigil''' was awarded to all valid entrants of the [https://www.warframe.com/news/the-tenno-s-greatest-trailer-contest Tenno's Greatest Trailer Contest].<br>{{SigilBox<br>\| mode        = stack<br>\| image       = TrailerEntrantSigil(SxWhite).png<br>\| name        = Trailer Entrant Sigil<br>\| description = Participatory prize for the Tenno's Greatest Trailer contest.<br>}}<br><br>The '''Lift Together Sigil''' is a limited-edition sigil awarded for redeeming the code "SOLARISUNITED" on the in-game market or the Warframe website. It was available starting October 23, 2018 until the code was retired sometime in early 2019.<br>{{SigilBox<br>\| mode        = stack<br>\| image       = LiftTogetherSigil.png<br>\| name        = Lift Together Sigil<br>\| description = A sigil depicting the mark of Solaris United.<br>}}<br><br>The '''Inktober Sigil''' was given to all eligible participants of the annual [[Halloween#Tennotober\|Tennotober]] event<br>{{SigilBox<br>\| mode        = stack<br>\| image       = InktoberGlyph(SxBlack).png<br>\| name        = Inktober Sigil<br>\| description = A sigil awarded for Inktober.<br>}}<br><br>The '''Conquera Sigil''' was given out during the [[Conquera\|Quest to Conquer Cancer]] charity event as a stretch goal reward with a [[Promo Codes\|promo-code]] (Conquer) from November 2nd, 2020 until January 11, 2021.<br>{{SigilBox<br>\| mode        = stack<br>\| image       = ConqueraSigil.png<br>\| link        = Conquera<br>\| name        = Conquera Sigil<br>\| description = Wear the Sigil as a symbol of support in the fight to conquer a disease that touches us all.<br>}}<br><br>The '''TennoGen Decennial Sigil''' was awarded for logging in between November, 11, 2025 to December, 31, 2025. <ref name="TennoGen Decennial">[https://www.warframe.com/news/tennogen-10-year-anniversary-celebration-and-sale We can't celebrate ten years of Customizations without a brand-new item for our wonderful Community! In your Inbox you'll find the TennoGen Decennial Sigil, Glyph, and Emblem as free Rewards.]</ref><br>{{SigilBox<br>\| mode        = stack<br>\| image       = TennoGenDecennialSigil(SxWhite).png<br>\| name        = TennoGen Decennial Sigil<br>\| description = A sigil commemorating ten years of TennoGen artistry.<br>}}<br><br>==Live Event Sigils==<br>These exclusive Sigils were given to attendees of TennoLive, TennoVIP and TennoCon.<br>{{SigilBox<br>\| image = TennoLive2015.png<br>\| name  = Tenno Live Sigil<br>}}<br>{{SigilBox<br>\| image = TennoLuminarySigil.png<br>\| name  = Tenno Luminary Sigil<br>}}<br>{{SigilBox<br>\| image = TennoCon2016Sigil.png<br>\| name  = TennoCon 2016 Sigil<br>}}<br>{{SigilBox<br>\| image = TennoCon2017Sigil.png<br>\| name  = TennoCon 2017 Sigil<br>}}<br>{{SigilBox<br>\| image = TennoCon2018Sigil.png<br>\| name  = TennoCon 2018 Sigil<br>}}<br>{{SigilBox<br>\| image = Tennocon2019Sigil.png<br>\| name  = TennoCon 2019 Sigil<br>}}<br>{{SigilBox<br>\| image = Tennocon2020Sigil.png<br>\| name  = TennoCon 2020 Sigil<br>}}<br>{{SigilBox<br>\| image = TennoCon2021Sigil.png<br>\| name  = TennoCon 2021 Sigil<br>}}<br>{{SigilBox<br>\| image = TennoCon2022Sigil.png<br>\| name  = TennoCon 2022 Sigil<br>}}<br>{{SigilBox<br>\| image = TennoCon2023Sigil.png<br>\| name  = TennoCon 2023 Sigil<br>}}<br>{{SigilBox<br>\| image = TennoCon2024Sigil.png<br>\| name  = TennoCon 2024 Sigil<br>}}<br>{{SigilBox<br>\| image = TennoCon2025Sigil.png<br>\| name  = TennoCon 2025 Sigil<br>}}<br>{{SigilBox<br>\| image = TennoVIP2025Sigil.png<br>\| name  = TennoVIP 2025 Sigil<br>}}<br>{{SigilBox<br>\| image = TennoCon2026Sigil(SxWhite).png<br>\| name  = TennoCon 2026 Sigil<br>}} |
| Nvidia Braton | `/Lotus/Upgrades/Skins/Nvidia/NvidiaBratonSkin` | ExportCustoms, Cosmetics | [page](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/Braton) | **Acquisition:** A fully built Braton can be purchased from the [[Market]] for {{cc\|25,000}}. |
